## Best date plans

### 2026-10-11 — Aparna Nancherla Is Trying…Again! + Ramen Danbo Park Slope

**Aparna Nancherla Is Trying…Again!, then dinner.** Ramen at Ramen Danbo Park Slope. It is a 5-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 5 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=702+Union+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=52+Seventh+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://unionhallny.com/calendar) · [Event booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/aparna-nancherla-is-tryingagain-tickets-1997802986512) · [Restaurant](https://ramendanbo.com/)

**Zine Class at Bryant Park Art Cart, then a meal.** Mexican at Oxomoco. It is 2.1 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 2:30 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.

#### [MoMA free Friday evening](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 46.7  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.

_No API key is needed for these public websites. `not checked` is distinct from a checked source with no final pick._

- **[arts] Balance — used:** largest source: Film Forum 11/26; Brooklyn 10/26. Largest source exceeds target because weekend film and comedy listings were the most fetchable official dated records; Brooklyn is below 40% by one item because mandatory Brooklyn Museum and Pioneer Works were blocked/unusable.
